# Patent Management System (PMS) - Complete Specifications


---
# DOCUMENT: 01_PMS_Problem_Statement_v1.2_SYNCHRONIZED.docx
---
Fusion ERP – Patent Management System (PMS)
Problem Statement
Synchronization note. The v1.1 frozen problem statement is preserved conservatively. One genuine terminology inconsistency was repaired: the isolated document-version state 'Awaiting Revision' is normalized to the established lifecycle state 'Needs Revision'. No new patent-management policy is introduced.
2. Synchronized Problem Statement
2.1 Overview
The Patent Management System (PMS) digitizes and streamlines institutional patent application management and intellectual property protection within Fusion ERP.
The module supports application submission, application documentation, review and decision workflows, revision and resubmission, attorney assignment, legal and patentability assessment, budget approval and financial tracking, external patent-office filing support, post-grant tracking, maintenance fee tracking, office-action response tracking, withdrawal, co-inventor and agreement management, prior-art search support, document version management, patent licensing and technology-transfer request tracking, appeal handling, deadline management, notifications, audit trails, and reporting.
PMS is intended to improve correctness, transparency, compliance support, timely communication, auditability, and operational coordination while reducing manual effort.
PMS records and tracks patent-management information. Legal decisions, patent-office decisions, actual payment execution, external accounting operations, and external patent-office processing remain outside PMS unless explicitly recorded, validated, notified, routed, logged, or reported within PMS.
2.2 Actors
2.2.1 Applicant
Submits new patent applications with required documentation.
Tracks application status and progress.
Revises and resubmits applications based on feedback.
Manages co-inventor information and agreements.
Conducts prior-art searches through PMS-supported search functions.
Withdraws applications when withdrawal is allowed by current application status.
Responds to office actions with attorney support.
Views personal application reports.
2.2.2 Director
Reviews assigned patent applications for technical merit.
Approves, rejects, or requests revisions to applications.
Provides detailed justification for decisions.
Reviews and approves budgets for patent filing and prosecution costs.
Makes final decisions on patent maintenance and renewal.
Views reports, dashboards, and analytics available within PMS.
Has oversight over application workflow in the Director domain.
2.2.3 PCC_ADMIN
Assigns applications to Directors based on explicit assignment criteria.
Assigns attorneys from approved panels, subject to stated exceptions.
Coordinates PMS operations and monitors workflow.
Resolves or routes conflict-of-interest and exceptional cases within PMS scope.
Manages PMS-specific configuration and role assignments.
Generates system-wide PMS reports.
Maintains attorney panels and expertise mappings.
2.2.4 Attorney
Conducts legal and patentability assessment.
Performs prior-art searches in relevant databases through PMS-supported search functions.
Provides legal opinions on patent applications.
Prepares applications for external filing.
Files applications with patent offices and records filing details in PMS.
Responds to office actions.
Manages post-grant tracking and maintenance-fee reminders/deadline tracking.
Provides legal advice throughout the patent lifecycle.
2.3 Functional Scope
2.3.1 Application Submission and Management
Applicants submit patent applications with required fields: title, abstract, detailed description, claims, inventor information, and supporting documents.
PMS validates that required application fields are complete before accepting submission.
PMS validates uploaded documents as PDF format, under 50 MB, and malware-free.
PMS classifies documents by sensitivity.
PMS assigns a unique application ID to each accepted application and sets the application status to Submitted.
Inventor agreements must be completed before filing.
PMS records application history and notifies relevant stakeholders.
2.3.2 Application Assignment and Routing
After submission, PCC_ADMIN or PMS automatic routing assigns applications to Directors.
Assignment uses Director expertise matching, workload balance, and conflict-of-interest checks.
Directors or PCC_ADMIN cannot review applications where they are listed as inventors.
PMS updates the application status to Under Review upon assignment.
PMS notifies the assigned Director and initiates the applicable review timeline.
2.3.3 Application Review and Decision
Directors review application details, documents, and supporting materials.
Directors must decide Approve, Reject, or Needs Revision.
Directors must provide detailed justification of at least 50 characters for all decisions.
Director review must be completed within 15 business days of assignment.
If an application is rejected or requires revision, PMS sends detailed feedback to the Applicant.
Approved applications proceed to attorney assignment.
PMS maintains an audit trail of the review process.
2.3.4 Application Revision and Resubmission
Applicants may revise applications with status Needs Revision or Rejected.
Applicants must resubmit revised applications within the applicable 60-day resubmission period from the relevant rejection/revision decision date.
PMS validates that a revision addresses Director feedback.
PMS maintains version control for revised applications and starts a new review cycle after resubmission.
If the resubmission deadline expires, PMS changes the application status to Expired.
Applicants may withdraw applications before the filing stage where current status allows withdrawal.
2.3.5 Attorney Assignment
Only PCC_ADMIN can assign attorneys to approved applications.
Attorneys must be selected from an approved panel unless an institutional exception is recorded or allowed.
Attorney assignment considers attorney expertise, workload, conflict-of-interest checks, and Applicant preference when the preferred attorney is in the approved panel.
The assigned Attorney must accept or decline the assignment.
PMS updates application status to Attorney Assigned or Pending Review as applicable.
2.3.6 Patentability Assessment and Legal Review
Attorney conducts legal and patentability analysis.
Attorney performs prior-art search in relevant databases.
Attorney evaluates novelty, non-obviousness, and utility.
Attorney records a detailed patentability opinion with recommendation: File Patent, Do Not File, or Needs Amendment.
PMS records the opinion and notifies relevant stakeholders.
Attorney may request additional information or extensions as supported by PMS.
2.3.7 Budget Approval and Financial Tracking
Director reviews and approves costs for patent filing and prosecution.
Budget information includes filing fees, attorney fees, and administrative costs.
If total cost exceeds the configured threshold limit, PMS routes/escalates the case to the applicable higher authority.
Budget approval is allowed only for applications with status Approved.
PMS validates budget availability before approval where budget availability information is available to PMS.
PMS logs financial transaction records and authorization levels for audit purposes.
Accounting, payment execution, and external finance-system processing are outside PMS unless recorded or integrated as PMS data.
2.3.8 External Filing and Patent Office Communication
Attorney prepares the application package according to applicable patent-office requirements.
PMS validates format compliance with applicable patent-office standards where validation rules are available to PMS.
Attorney submits the application to the Indian Patent Office or an international patent office.
International filings require additional justification to be recorded in PMS.
PMS captures external filing ID, filing date, and confirmation information.
PMS updates application status to Filed after filing is recorded.
PMS logs official communications with patent offices.
2.3.9 Post-Grant Tracking and Maintenance
Post-grant tracking activates only for patents with status Granted.
PMS calculates maintenance-fee schedules based on grant date and jurisdiction where the applicable schedule rules are available to PMS.
Attorney manages maintenance-fee reminders and deadline tracking in PMS.
Before each maintenance deadline, an explicit renewal decision is required.
Attorney seeks renewal decision from the Director.
Director decides Renew or Do Not Renew.
If renewal is approved, Attorney processes the maintenance action and records the transaction/status in PMS.
PMS maintains renewal history and schedules the next maintenance cycle.
2.3.10 Office Actions and Responses
PMS records office actions received from patent offices.
Attorney reviews office actions and prepares draft responses.
Applicant reviews and approves a response when the applicable process requires Applicant review.
Attorney finalizes and submits responses within applicable deadlines.
Attorney files extension requests when additional time is needed and the process allows extension.
PMS tracks response submission and updates status.
PMS automates timeline and deadline tracking for office-action responses where applicable deadline data is available.
2.3.11 Application Withdrawal
Applicants may withdraw applications before the filing stage.
PMS validates that withdrawal is allowed based on current application status.
Applicant must provide a withdrawal reason.
PMS records co-inventor consent when the applicable process requires consent.
PMS updates application status to Withdrawn and removes the application from the active queue.
PMS maintains withdrawal history.
2.3.12 Co-Inventor and Agreement Management
Applicants manage multiple inventors and ownership shares.
PMS validates inventor information and ownership percentages.
PMS collects and validates inventor consent and electronic signatures where legally permitted and configured.
Inventor agreements must be completed before filing.
PMS sends notifications to affected parties when inventor information changes.
2.3.13 Prior Art Search
Applicants and Attorneys can search for prior-art references through PMS-supported search facilities.
PMS queries relevant patent and publication databases where integrations or search access are available.
PMS displays search results with relevance ranking.
Users can attach selected references to an application.
PMS logs search sessions for audit trail.
PMS supports keyword optimization and classification mapping as part of the search process.
2.3.14 Document Version Management
PMS maintains version history of application documents.
Users can upload new document versions where their role and application status permit upload.
PMS applies automatic version numbering.
Previous document versions are archived and accessible according to role and status access rules.
PMS logs document modifications with user and timestamp.
Document access is controlled based on user role and application status.
Applicants can edit documents only when application status is Needs Revision.
2.3.15 Patent Licensing and Technology Transfer
Applicants can submit licensing or technology-transfer requests.
PMS routes such requests to the appropriate committee or Director as stated by the applicable institutional process.
PMS tracks negotiation process information.
PMS records final agreements.
PMS updates or shares financial-system information where integration is available.
PMS maintains license records with confidentiality controls.
2.3.16 Appeal Process
Applicants may file appeals against rejected applications.
Appeals must be filed within the applicable allowed time period.
Applicant provides grounds and justification for appeal.
Director reviews appeal and supporting documentation.
Director may Uphold Rejection, Remand for Amendment, or Modify Decision.
PMS records the appeal outcome and initiates the next applicable process step.
2.3.17 Deadline Management and Tracking
PMS calculates and monitors patent-management deadlines where deadline rules are available.
Deadlines include review deadlines, revision deadlines, office-action response deadlines, and maintenance-fee deadlines.
PMS generates alerts at configurable intervals, including 30 days, 15 days, and 5 days before deadline where applicable.
PMS sends deadline notifications to relevant stakeholders.
Missed deadlines trigger escalations according to the configured escalation chain.
PMS logs deadline-tracking activities.
2.3.18 Reports and Analytics
PCC_ADMIN and Directors can generate PMS reports.
Available reports include application status and progress reports, financial and budget reports, deadline and pending-action reports, inventor and co-inventor reports, patent portfolio analysis, and workload and assignment reports.
PMS supports scheduled reports, exportable reports, and customizable reports as stated in the source baseline.
PMS logs report access for audit purposes.
PMS provides dashboards, historical data analysis, trend reporting, and workload analytics within the explicitly stated PMS reporting scope.
2.3.19 Notifications
PMS issues system-generated email notifications for application submission and status changes.
PMS issues notifications for assignment to Director or Attorney.
PMS issues notifications for review decisions: approve, reject, or revision.
PMS issues notifications for budget approval or rejection.
PMS issues notifications for filing confirmations and office actions.
PMS issues notifications for deadline reminders and escalations.
PMS issues notifications for maintenance-fee reminders.
PMS issues notifications for document updates and version changes.
PMS notification triggering is within module scope; central email delivery infrastructure is outside PMS scope.
2.3.20 Audit Trail and Compliance
PMS maintains audit trails of PMS actions.
PMS logs user actions, status changes, and document modifications.
PMS logs include user ID, action type, timestamp, application ID, previous state, and new state where applicable.
Critical operations create immutable audit records.
Audit logs support compliance verification and forensic analysis.
PMS supports document sensitivity classification, role-based access control, and version control for confidential patent information.
PMS applies data encryption at rest and in transit where this security requirement is implemented by the platform/module boundary.
2.3.21 PMS Role, Workflow, Integration, and System Boundaries
Exactly one active PCC_ADMIN oversees PMS.
Multiple Directors handle reviews based on expertise domains.
Multiple Attorneys from approved panels conduct legal assessments.
Multiple Applicants submit applications.
PMS supports automated routing based on configurable rules, status-driven workflow, escalation mechanisms for delayed actions, and parallel processing where explicitly applicable.
PMS may integrate with patent office systems, financial systems, email notification systems, and document management systems where available.
External systems remain outside PMS unless PMS explicitly validates, routes, records, notifies, logs, or reports based on their data or events.
The System is not treated as a regular primary actor in later use cases derived from this problem statement. System behavior appears as internal validation, routing, status update, deadline calculation, notification, escalation, postcondition, maintained data, or reporting behavior.
2.4 Scope Boundaries
PMS does not own HR master data, payroll, platform-wide user-account administration, procurement execution, payment execution, external accounting operations, patent-office decision making, external legal decisions, or external database operation.
PMS may reference external policies, laws, patent-office requirements, jurisdiction schedules, and institutional committees only where PMS explicitly validates, restricts, routes, records, notifies, escalates, or reports based on them.
PMS does not define the cost-escalation threshold, office-action response deadlines, jurisdiction maintenance schedules, exact appeal deadline, exact authorization hierarchy, legal/e-signature validity rules, or external patent-office standards unless such values are configured in PMS.
Non-functional items in the source statement are retained only where they create explicit system-relevant functional behavior, such as audit logging, role-based access control, malware validation, document version control, notifications, reporting, deadline tracking, and encryption boundary statements.
End of Synchronized Problem Statement

| Version   | v1.2       | Status           | SYNCHRONIZED                    |
|:----------|:-----------|:-----------------|:--------------------------------|
| Date      | 2026-08-22 | Governing Method | PS → FR → UC + BR → BW/CW → RTM |

# DOCUMENT: 02_PMS_Requirement_Registry_CFR_AFR_v1.3_DIRECT_FINAL.docx
---
Fusion ERP – Patent Management System (PMS)
Requirement Registry (CFR + AFR)
1. Registry Basis and Continuity
This v1.3 registry converts the synchronized v1.2 atomic Functional Requirement Registry into the DIRECT_FINAL_v2 two-level Requirement Registry without changing the accepted atomic obligation wording.
The 203 active v1.2 PMS-FR-nnn obligations are retained verbatim as historical continuity evidence and mapped one-to-one to PMS-AFR-nnn identifiers with the same numeric suffix. No historical requirement is retrospectively rewritten.
CFRs consolidate related AFRs into independently understandable obligations. AFR decomposition is retained only because separate verification, traceability, policy checking, workflow mapping, or implementation follow-up is materially useful.
Precise traceability is maintained at AFR level and rolled up to the parent CFR. Minimum sufficient representation uses only UC, BR, WF, UC+BR, UC+WF, BR+WF, or UC+BR+WF.
2. Coherent / Consolidated Requirements (CFR)
3. Atomic Requirements (AFR) and Traceability

# DOCUMENT: 03_PMS_Use_Case_Specifications_v1.3_DIRECT_FINAL.docx
---
Fusion ERP – Patent Management System (PMS)
Use Case Specifications
1. Use Case Conventions
The synchronized specification contains 33 actor-triggered use cases. UC-PMS-001 through UC-PMS-028 retain the stable legacy IDs where the goal remains valid; UC-PMS-029 through UC-PMS-033 are newly added goals required for semantic completeness.
The System is not modeled as a regular primary actor. Automatic routing, validation, deadline calculation, notification, audit creation, and integration behavior appear inside actor-goal use cases or as rules/postconditions. No separate Scheduled Function artifact is used; no current PMS use case requires event-triggered reclassification because the accepted automatic behaviours remain subordinate to actor-goal services or workflow guards/postconditions.
Temporal succession between independent actor goals is represented in workflows, not through UML include/extend links.
2. Common Cross-Cutting Behaviour
Role/access: PMS-AFR-216 / BR-PMS-126 applies to all use cases; confidential patent information is further constrained by PMS-AFR-220 / BR-PMS-130 where applicable.
Audit: PMS-AFR-207 through PMS-AFR-212 apply to relevant user/state/document-changing actions. UC-PMS-028 is the actor goal for viewing audit/compliance logs; audit-record creation remains internal behaviour of the affected use cases.
Notifications: event-specific notification FRs are represented in the postconditions/flows of the use cases that create those events. BR-PMS-116 preserves the boundary that PMS triggers email while external delivery infrastructure is outside PMS.
Security boundary: PMS-AFR-214 and PMS-AFR-215 apply across PMS data handling where encryption is implemented at the module/platform boundary.
Status-driven behaviour: PMS-AFR-225 applies to state-changing use cases and is made explicit in the Basis/Composite workflow state-transition tables.
3. Use Case Specifications
UC-PMS-001 — Submit Patent Application
UC-PMS-002 — Track Application Status and History
UC-PMS-003 — Manage Co-Inventors and Agreements
UC-PMS-004 — Search and Attach Prior Art
UC-PMS-005 — Revise and Resubmit Application
UC-PMS-006 — Withdraw Patent Application
UC-PMS-007 — Assign Application to Director
UC-PMS-008 — Review Patent Application
UC-PMS-009 — Review and Decide Patent Budget
UC-PMS-010 — Assign Attorney to Approved Application
UC-PMS-011 — Accept or Decline Attorney Assignment
UC-PMS-012 — Conduct Patentability Assessment
UC-PMS-013 — Prepare and Record External Patent Filing
UC-PMS-014 — Record Patent Office Communication
UC-PMS-015 — Respond to Office Action
UC-PMS-016 — Monitor Deadlines and Escalations
UC-PMS-017 — Manage Post-Grant Tracking and Maintenance
UC-PMS-018 — Make Maintenance Renewal Decision
UC-PMS-019 — Manage Document Versions
UC-PMS-020 — Submit Licensing or Technology-Transfer Request
UC-PMS-021 — Review Licensing or Technology-Transfer Request
UC-PMS-022 — File Appeal Against Rejection
UC-PMS-023 — Review Appeal
UC-PMS-024 — Generate System-Wide PMS Reports and Analytics
UC-PMS-025 — Manage PMS Roles and Configuration
UC-PMS-026 — Maintain Attorney Panel and Expertise Mappings
UC-PMS-027 — Monitor Workflow and Handle Exceptional Cases
UC-PMS-028 — View Audit Trail and Compliance Logs
UC-PMS-029 — Review Office-Action Response
UC-PMS-030 — View Own Application Reports
UC-PMS-031 — View PMS Reports and Analytics
UC-PMS-032 — View Application Workflow Oversight
UC-PMS-033 — Record Patent Legal Advice

| Version   | v1.3       | Status           | DIRECT_FINAL                         |
|:----------|:-----------|:-----------------|:-------------------------------------|
| Date      | 2026-08-30 | Governing Method | PS → CFR/AFR → UC + BR → BW/CW → RTM |


| UC Type                     | Actor-triggered                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
|:----------------------------|:-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| Primary Actor               | Applicant                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Supporting Actors / Systems | PMS internal validation; external malware-scanning service if configured                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Goal / Description          | Create and submit a complete patent application for institutional processing.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Trigger                     | Applicant selects the new-application submission function.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Preconditions               | Applicant has a valid PMS role. A submission draft can be created.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Main Success Scenario       | 1. Applicant enters title, abstract, detailed description, claims, inventor information, and supporting documents. 2. PMS validates required-field completeness and validates each submitted document as PDF, under 50 MB, and malware-free. 3. PMS classifies accepted documents by sensitivity. 4. Applicant confirms submission. 5. PMS accepts the application, assigns a unique application ID, sets status to Submitted, records history/audit data, and triggers the submission notification. |
| Alternative Flows           | • If required information is incomplete, PMS identifies the missing information and keeps the application unsubmitted. • If a document fails format, size, or malware validation, PMS rejects that document and keeps the valid prior application state.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Exception Flows             | • If an external validation dependency is unavailable, PMS does not treat the application as successfully accepted unless the required validation has completed.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| Success Postcondition       | An accepted application has a unique ID and status Submitted; its submission/history/audit record exists.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Failure / Minimal Guarantee | No invalid submission is accepted; the prior valid draft/application state is retained.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Related Business Rules      | BR-PMS-014; BR-PMS-015; BR-PMS-016; BR-PMS-017; BR-PMS-126; BR-PMS-130                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| Related CFRs / AFRs         |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Related Workflow(s)         | BW-PMS-01; CW-PMS-01                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Notes / TODO                | Inventor agreements are a filing prerequisite, not a submission prerequisite.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |


| UC Type                     | Actor-triggered                                                                                                                                                 |
|:----------------------------|:----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| Primary Actor               | Applicant                                                                                                                                                       |
| Supporting Actors / Systems | None                                                                                                                                                            |
| Goal / Description          | View the current status, progress, and recorded history of the Applicant's own applications.                                                                    |
| Trigger                     | Applicant opens an application or personal application-tracking view.                                                                                           |
| Preconditions               | Applicant has a valid PMS role and access to the selected application.                                                                                          |
| Main Success Scenario       | 1. Applicant selects one of their patent applications. 2. PMS displays current status/progress and the recorded application history available to the Applicant. |
| Alternative Flows           | • Applicant may navigate among their own applications and available history entries.                                                                            |
| Exception Flows             | • If the Applicant is not authorized for the selected record, PMS blocks access.                                                                                |
| Success Postcondition       | The Applicant has viewed current status/progress/history; no business state changes.                                                                            |
| Failure / Minimal Guarantee | No application data is modified.                                                                                                                                |

| UC Type                     | Actor-triggered                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
|:----------------------------|:--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| Primary Actor               | Applicant                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Supporting Actors / Systems | Co-inventors are recorded parties, not modeled PMS actors                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Goal / Description          | Maintain inventor identities, ownership shares, consents, and agreements for an application.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| Trigger                     | Applicant opens inventor/agreement management for an application.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| Preconditions               | Applicant has access to the application and the current role/status permits the requested change.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| Main Success Scenario       | 1. Applicant adds or updates inventor information and ownership shares. 2. PMS validates inventor information and ownership percentages. 3. Where required, PMS records inventor consent and, where legally permitted and configured, collects/validates electronic signatures. 4. PMS records agreement information and notifies affected parties when inventor information changes. |
| Alternative Flows           | • Applicant may update the inventor set or ownership shares before filing, subject to applicable access/state rules.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| Exception Flows             | • Invalid inventor information, ownership percentages, consent, or signature validation prevents the affected change from being accepted.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Success Postcondition       | Valid inventor/agreement information is stored; agreement-completion state is available for the filing prerequisite.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| Failure / Minimal Guarantee | Invalid changes are not committed; the prior valid inventor/agreement data remains available.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |

| Alternative Flows           | • Actor may refine keywords/classification mapping and repeat the search.                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |

| UC Type                     | Actor-triggered                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
|:----------------------------|:-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| Primary Actor               | Applicant                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Supporting Actors / Systems | Director (feedback source)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Goal / Description          | Revise an application after a Needs Revision or Rejected decision and resubmit it for a new review cycle.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Trigger                     | Applicant selects revise/resubmit for an eligible application.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Preconditions               | Application status is Needs Revision or Rejected; the 60-day resubmission period has not expired.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| Main Success Scenario       | 1. Applicant reviews the Director feedback. 2. Applicant edits permitted application content/documents and uploads revised versions. 3. PMS validates that the resubmission addresses the Director feedback and applies document/version rules. 4. Applicant submits the revision. 5. PMS records the revised version and starts a new review cycle. |
| Alternative Flows           | • The Applicant may save revisions before final resubmission while the permitted revision state remains active.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Exception Flows             | • If the deadline has expired, PMS changes the application to Expired and does not accept a late resubmission. • If the submitted revision does not address the recorded Director feedback, PMS rejects the resubmission and retains the prior valid state.                                                                                          |
| Success Postcondition       | A revised application is recorded for a new review cycle.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Failure / Minimal Guarantee | No invalid/late resubmission is accepted.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Related Business Rules      | BR-PMS-033; BR-PMS-034; BR-PMS-035; BR-PMS-036; BR-PMS-038; BR-PMS-082; BR-PMS-087; BR-PMS-126                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Related CFRs / AFRs         |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

| UC Type                     | Actor-triggered                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
|:----------------------------|:----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| Primary Actor               | Applicant                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| Supporting Actors / Systems | PCC_ADMIN (operational visibility only)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Goal / Description          | Withdraw an application when the applicable pre-filing status permits withdrawal.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Trigger                     | Applicant selects withdraw for an application.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| Preconditions               | Application has not reached Filed status and the current application status is configured/defined as withdrawal-eligible.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| Main Success Scenario       | 1. PMS checks that withdrawal is allowed for the current application status and before filing. 2. Applicant provides a withdrawal reason. 3. Where the applicable process requires it, PMS records co-inventor consent. 4. Applicant confirms withdrawal. 5. PMS sets status to Withdrawn, removes the application from the active queue, and records withdrawal history/status change. |
| Alternative Flows           | • None beyond the conditional co-inventor-consent requirement.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| Exception Flows             | • If the current status is not withdrawal-eligible, PMS blocks withdrawal and retains the current state.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Success Postcondition       | The application is Withdrawn and no longer appears in the active queue.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Failure / Minimal Guarantee | The application remains in its prior valid state if withdrawal is not permitted.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

| UC Type                     | Actor-triggered                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
|:----------------------------|:-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| Primary Actor               | PCC_ADMIN                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

PMS prevents assignment to a Director who is listed as an inventor; PCC_ADMIN cannot act as reviewer where they are an inventor. 3. PCC_ADMIN confirms the selected Director, or PMS performs configured automatic routing using the same rules. 4. PMS records the assignment, sets status to Under Review, notifies the Director, and initiates the review deadline. |
| Alternative Flows           | • Automatic routing may perform the assignment without treating the System as a primary actor.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Exception Flows             | • If no eligible assignment is available, PMS leaves the application Submitted and records/raises the exceptional case for PCC_ADMIN handling.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Success Postcondition       | The application is assigned and status is Under Review, or remains Submitted if assignment is blocked.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Failure / Minimal Guarantee | No invalid/conflicted assignment is committed.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

| UC Type                     | Actor-triggered                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
|:----------------------------|:-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| Primary Actor               | Director                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| Supporting Actors / Systems | Applicant; PCC_ADMIN                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Goal / Description          | Review an assigned application and record one supported technical-merit decision.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Trigger                     | Director opens an assigned application in Under Review.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| Preconditions               | Director is the assigned, non-conflicted reviewer; the application is Under Review.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Main Success Scenario       | 1. Director reviews application details, documents, and supporting materials. 2. Director selects exactly one outcome: Approve, Reject, or Needs Revision. 3. Director enters a detailed justification of at least 50 characters. 4. Director submits the decision within the 15-business-day review period. 5. PMS records the decision/audit trail. Approved applications become eligible for attorney assignment; Reject/Needs Revision decisions trigger detailed feedback to the Applicant. |
| Alternative Flows           | • Reject and Needs Revision are valid alternative outcomes to Approve.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Exception Flows             | • A decision without the required justification is not accepted. • A conflicted/unassigned Director is not allowed to submit the review decision.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Success Postcondition       | One review outcome is recorded with justification and audit history.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Failure / Minimal Guarantee | No invalid review decision changes the application state.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

| Notes / TODO                | N/A                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |


| UC Type                     | Actor-triggered                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
|:----------------------------|:-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| Primary Actor               | Director                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Supporting Actors / Systems | PCC_ADMIN; applicable higher authority is an external/configured boundary                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Goal / Description          | Review filing/prosecution costs and record a budget decision or escalation.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| Trigger                     | Budget review is requested for an Approved application.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Preconditions               | Application status is Approved.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Main Success Scenario       | 1. PMS presents filing fees, attorney fees, administrative costs, and available budget information where such data is available. 2. Director reviews the proposed cost. 3. If the cost is within the configured threshold, Director records the budget decision. 4. If the cost exceeds the configured threshold, PMS routes/escalates the case to the applicable higher authority. 5. PMS records financial/authorization metadata and triggers the applicable budget notification. |
| Alternative Flows           | • Within-threshold outcome may be Budget Approved or Budget Rejected. • Above-threshold cases result in Escalated rather than an invented higher-authority decision.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Exception Flows             | • If budget-availability data is required and available but validation fails, PMS does not record approval.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| Success Postcondition       | Budget decision or escalation state is recorded with audit/financial metadata.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Failure / Minimal Guarantee | No approval is recorded when required constraints fail.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |

| Related CFRs / AFRs         |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Related Workflow(s)         | BW-PMS-08; BW-PMS-09; BW-PMS-10; CW-PMS-02                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Notes / TODO                | The exact escalation authority and threshold value remain configured/external policy values.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |


| UC Type                     | Actor-triggered                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
|:----------------------------|:---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| Primary Actor               | PCC_ADMIN                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

| Preconditions               | Application status is Approved; PCC_ADMIN is authorized.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

PMS blocks a non-panel attorney unless a recorded institutional exception permits the assignment. 3. PCC_ADMIN selects and records the Attorney assignment. 4. PMS records the assignment and notifies the Attorney for acceptance/decline. |
| Alternative Flows           | • A preferred Attorney is considered only when that Attorney is in the approved panel. • An approved institutional exception may permit a non-panel attorney.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Exception Flows             | • If conflict-of-interest or other assignment constraints fail, PMS does not record the assignment.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Success Postcondition       | An Attorney assignment is recorded and is awaiting/subject to the Attorney response.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| Failure / Minimal Guarantee | No invalid assignment is committed.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

| UC Type                     | Actor-triggered                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
|:----------------------------|:-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|

| Main Success Scenario       | 1. Attorney reviews the assignment. 2. Attorney chooses Accept or Decline. 3. PMS records the response. 4. On acceptance, PMS records the assignment as accepted/Attorney Assigned; on decline, PMS records Pending Review as applicable and notifies PCC_ADMIN. |
| Alternative Flows           | • Accept and Decline are the two supported outcomes.                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Exception Flows             | • If no active assignment exists for the Attorney, PMS does not accept a response.                                                                                                                                                                               |

| UC Type                     | Actor-triggered                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
|:----------------------------|:--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| Primary Actor               | Attorney                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Supporting Actors / Systems | Applicant; Director                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

| Preconditions               | Attorney has the assigned application and required access.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| Main Success Scenario       | 1. Attorney reviews the application and performs/records relevant prior-art research. 2. Attorney evaluates novelty, non-obviousness, and utility. 3. Attorney records a detailed patentability opinion. 4. Attorney selects one recommendation: File Patent, Do Not File, or Needs Amendment. 5. PMS records the opinion and notifies relevant stakeholders. |
| Alternative Flows           | • Attorney may request additional information or an extension where PMS/the applicable process supports it.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Exception Flows             | • If the opinion does not contain one supported recommendation, PMS does not finalize the opinion.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Success Postcondition       | A patentability opinion and recommendation are recorded.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Failure / Minimal Guarantee | The prior valid legal-review record remains if finalization fails.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
