| UC Type                     | Actor-triggered                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
|:----------------------------|:-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| Primary Actor               | Attorney                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| Supporting Actors / Systems | External patent office; Director/PCC_ADMIN data as prerequisites                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Goal / Description          | Prepare a filing package, perform supported validation, submit externally, and record filing confirmation in PMS.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Trigger                     | Attorney starts filing for an application ready for external filing.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Preconditions               | Required approvals and budget allocation are complete; inventor agreements are complete; Attorney has filing authority/access.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Main Success Scenario       | 1. Attorney prepares the application package. 2. Where validation rules are available, PMS validates the package against applicable patent-office standards. 3. For an international filing, Attorney records the required additional justification. 4. Attorney submits the package to the selected patent office outside PMS. 5. Attorney/PMS records the external filing ID, filing date, and confirmation. 6. PMS sets application status to Filed, records audit/history, and triggers filing confirmation. |
| Alternative Flows           | • National and international filings use their applicable requirements; international filing additionally requires justification.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Exception Flows             | • If a required approval/budget/agreement prerequisite is missing, PMS blocks filing. • If external submission or confirmation is not obtained, PMS does not represent the filing as successfully Filed.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| Success Postcondition       | External filing details are recorded and application status is Filed.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Failure / Minimal Guarantee | No false external filing success is recorded.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

| Related CFRs / AFRs         |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

| UC Type                     | Actor-triggered                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

If the communication is an office action, PMS creates/updates the office-action tracking information and triggers the office-action notification. |
| Alternative Flows           | • Non-office-action official communications are recorded without initiating an office-action response path.                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Exception Flows             | • Invalid/unrelated communication data is not attached to another application.                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| Success Postcondition       | The official communication is recorded; office actions are available for response tracking.                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Failure / Minimal Guarantee | No unrelated application state is changed.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |

| UC Type                     | Actor-triggered                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
|:----------------------------|:--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| Primary Actor               | Attorney                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| Supporting Actors / Systems | Applicant (when review is required); external patent office                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Goal / Description          | Prepare, finalize, and record submission of an office-action response within applicable timing rules.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Trigger                     | Attorney opens a recorded office action requiring response.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Preconditions               | Office action exists; Attorney has access; applicable deadline/extension data is available where required.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| Main Success Scenario       | 1. Attorney reviews the office action and prepares a draft response. 2. If Applicant review is required, PMS makes the draft available for UC-PMS-029 and records the response as awaiting Applicant approval. 3. If Applicant review is not required, or after required Applicant approval is recorded, Attorney finalizes the response. 4. Attorney submits the response externally and records submission/status in PMS. 5. PMS updates response tracking and audit/history information. |
| Alternative Flows           | • If additional time is needed and the applicable process permits an extension, Attorney records/files an extension request.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Exception Flows             | • PMS does not mark a response submitted unless the required Applicant approval (when applicable) and submission record exist. • A missed deadline is handled by the deadline/escalation rules; PMS does not invent a legal outcome.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Success Postcondition       | Response submission/extension status is recorded.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Failure / Minimal Guarantee | No unsupported response submission or legal outcome is recorded.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

| UC Type                     | Actor-triggered                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
|:----------------------------|:--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| Primary Actor               | PCC_ADMIN                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Supporting Actors / Systems | Applicant; Director; Attorney                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| Goal / Description          | Monitor calculated deadlines, reminders, missed-deadline escalations, and pending actions.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Trigger                     | PCC_ADMIN opens the deadline/pending-action monitoring view.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| Preconditions               | PCC_ADMIN has an active authorized role; deadline records may exist.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| Main Success Scenario       | 1. PMS calculates/maintains applicable review, revision, office-action, and maintenance deadlines using available rules/data. 2. PMS generates configured 30/15/5-day alerts where applicable and triggers notifications. 3. PCC_ADMIN reviews upcoming/missed deadlines and escalation records. 4. PCC_ADMIN routes or handles delayed-action exceptional cases within PMS scope. 5. PMS logs deadline-tracking/escalation activity. |
| Alternative Flows           | • Where deadline rules/data are unavailable, PMS does not fabricate a deadline; the item remains flagged as requiring applicable data/configuration.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| Exception Flows             | • A missed deadline may be escalated according to the configured chain; legal consequences outside PMS are not inferred.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Success Postcondition       | Deadline and escalation records are current and visible to authorized actors.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| Failure / Minimal Guarantee | No external/legal deadline outcome is invented.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Related Business Rules      | BR-PMS-097; BR-PMS-098; BR-PMS-099; BR-PMS-100; BR-PMS-101; BR-PMS-102; BR-PMS-103; BR-PMS-104; BR-PMS-116; BR-PMS-126                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Related CFRs / AFRs         |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Related Workflow(s)         | N/A                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Notes / TODO                | Most deadline transitions are PMS-internal rule-driven behavior, not separate actor-goal workflow activities.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |


| UC Type                     | Actor-triggered                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
|:----------------------------|:--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| Primary Actor               | Attorney                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| Supporting Actors / Systems | Director                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| Goal / Description          | Track granted patents, maintenance schedules, renewal decisions, and recorded maintenance actions.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| Trigger                     | A patent is Granted or a maintenance deadline approaches.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Preconditions               | Patent status is Granted for post-grant tracking.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Main Success Scenario       | 1. PMS activates post-grant tracking and calculates the applicable maintenance schedule where schedule rules/data are available. 2. Attorney monitors the upcoming maintenance deadline and requests an explicit renewal decision from the Director. 3. After a Renew decision is recorded through UC-PMS-018, Attorney processes/records the maintenance action, transaction information, and resulting status. 4. PMS maintains renewal history and schedules the next maintenance cycle. |
| Alternative Flows           | • If the Director records Do Not Renew, no renewal maintenance action is recorded as approved.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Exception Flows             | • If schedule rules/data are unavailable, PMS does not fabricate a maintenance schedule.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| Success Postcondition       | Post-grant maintenance state/history is updated according to the Director decision.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| Failure / Minimal Guarantee | No unapproved maintenance action is recorded as approved.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Related Business Rules      | BR-PMS-059; BR-PMS-060; BR-PMS-061; BR-PMS-100; BR-PMS-126                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| Related CFRs / AFRs         |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Related Workflow(s)         | BW-PMS-14; BW-PMS-15; CW-PMS-04                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Notes / TODO                | Actual payment execution is outside PMS; PMS records the maintenance action/transaction/status.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |


| UC Type                     | Actor-triggered                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
|:----------------------------|:--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| Primary Actor               | Director                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Supporting Actors / Systems | Attorney                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Goal / Description          | Record the required Renew or Do Not Renew decision for an upcoming maintenance deadline.                                                                                                                                        |
| Trigger                     | Attorney requests a renewal decision before a maintenance deadline.                                                                                                                                                             |
| Preconditions               | Patent is in post-grant tracking and a renewal decision is due.                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Main Success Scenario       | 1. Director reviews the patent and available maintenance information. 2. Director chooses Renew or Do Not Renew. 3. PMS records the decision and makes it available to the Attorney for the next applicable maintenance action. |
| Alternative Flows           | • Renew and Do Not Renew are the supported outcomes.                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Exception Flows             | • If the patent is not in the applicable Granted/post-grant context, PMS does not accept the renewal decision.                                                                                                                  |
| Success Postcondition       | One renewal decision is recorded for the maintenance cycle.                                                                                                                                                                     |
| Failure / Minimal Guarantee | No invalid decision is recorded.                                                                                                                                                                                                |

| UC Type                     | Actor-triggered                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |

| Exception Flows             | • If the applicable routing target/process cannot be determined from configuration/policy, PMS does not invent a committee decision.                                                                                                                                              |

| Failure / Minimal Guarantee | No unsupported review outcome is recorded.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

| UC Type                     | Actor-triggered                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

| Exception Flows             | • PMS does not invent approval/rejection semantics that are not stated in the frozen PS.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |

| Goal / Description          | Generate/view PMS reports and analytics within the Director's authorized scope.                                                                                              |
| Trigger                     | Director selects a report/dashboard function.                                                                                                                                |
| Preconditions               | Director has an authorized PMS role.                                                                                                                                         |
| Main Success Scenario       | 1. Director selects an available report/analytics view. 2. PMS generates the output using PMS data within the Director's authorized scope/domain. 3. PMS logs report access. |
| Alternative Flows           | • Where authorized, scheduled/export/customized report functions may be used.                                                                                                |
| Exception Flows             | • Unauthorized report scope is blocked.                                                                                                                                      |
| Success Postcondition       | Director views authorized report/analytics output.                                                                                                                           |
| Failure / Minimal Guarantee | No business state changes.                                                                                                                                                   |
| Related Business Rules      | BR-PMS-005; BR-PMS-006; BR-PMS-126                                                                                                                                           |
| Related CFRs / AFRs         |                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Related Workflow(s)         | N/A                                                                                                                                                                          |
| Notes / TODO                | Added to separate the Director reporting goal from PCC_ADMIN system-wide reporting.                                                                                          |


| UC Type                     | Actor-triggered                                                                                                                                                        |
|:----------------------------|:-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| Primary Actor               | Director                                                                                                                                                               |
| Supporting Actors / Systems | None                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Goal / Description          | View workflow oversight information for applications in the Director's domain.                                                                                         |
| Trigger                     | Director opens workflow oversight/dashboard information.                                                                                                               |
| Preconditions               | Director has an authorized role and domain scope.                                                                                                                      |
| Main Success Scenario       | 1. Director selects the relevant workflow/application scope. 2. PMS displays workflow states, pending actions, and oversight information within the Director's domain. |
| Alternative Flows           | • None.                                                                                                                                                                |
| Exception Flows             | • Out-of-domain information is not disclosed.                                                                                                                          |
| Success Postcondition       | Director views domain-scoped workflow oversight.                                                                                                                       |
| Failure / Minimal Guarantee | No business state changes.                                                                                                                                             |
| Related Business Rules      | BR-PMS-006; BR-PMS-126                                                                                                                                                 |
| Related CFRs / AFRs         |                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Related Workflow(s)         | N/A                                                                                                                                                                    |
| Notes / TODO                | Added to preserve the explicit Director oversight goal.                                                                                                                |


| UC Type                     | Actor-triggered                                                                                                                                                                                   |
|:----------------------------|:--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| Primary Actor               | Attorney                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| Supporting Actors / Systems | Applicant; Director                                                                                                                                                                               |
| Goal / Description          | Record legal advice associated with a patent application throughout its lifecycle.                                                                                                                |
| Trigger                     | Attorney records legal advice for an application.                                                                                                                                                 |
| Preconditions               | Attorney has a valid role and access to the application.                                                                                                                                          |
| Main Success Scenario       | 1. Attorney selects the application/context. 2. Attorney records the legal advice note/opinion information. 3. PMS associates the advice with the application history/audit record as applicable. |
| Alternative Flows           | • This use case may occur at different lifecycle stages; it does not itself impose a new application-state transition.                                                                            |
| Exception Flows             | • Unauthorized access or invalid application reference prevents the record from being stored.                                                                                                     |
| Success Postcondition       | Legal advice information is recorded for the application.                                                                                                                                         |
| Failure / Minimal Guarantee | No unrelated application state changes.                                                                                                                                                           |
| Related Business Rules      | BR-PMS-126; BR-PMS-130                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Related CFRs / AFRs         |                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Related Workflow(s)         | N/A                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| Notes / TODO                | Added to preserve the explicit Attorney lifecycle-advice capability without forcing it into patentability assessment.                                                                             |

# DOCUMENT: 04_PMS_Business_Rule_Specifications_v1.3_DIRECT_FINAL.docx
---
Fusion ERP – Patent Management System (PMS)
Business Rule Specifications
1. Business Rule Conventions
The synchronized baseline retains 84 genuine governing rules. Ordinary system functions are not duplicated as BRs merely to increase coverage.
Rules are used for eligibility, validation, authorization/access, routing/assignment, decision constraints, timing/deadlines, calculations/data conventions, lifecycle constraints, and explicit integration/scope boundaries.
Legacy BR IDs are preserved when the underlying rule remains valid. BR-PMS-146 is new and represents the explicit condition governing Applicant review of office-action responses.

# DOCUMENT: 05_PMS_Workflow_Specifications_v1.3_DIRECT_FINAL.docx
---
Fusion ERP – Patent Management System (PMS)
Workflow Specifications
1. Workflow Method
The synchronized workflow package contains 16 Basis Workflows and 5 Composite Workflows. The state-transition table is authoritative; diagrams are secondary views.
Workflow steps are grounded in validated state-changing actor goals. Informational/search/report/audit-view use cases remain use cases but are not forced into workflow steps.
A BR constrains a transition through its guard/condition; a BR is not represented as an activity.
No single all-inclusive Complete Workflow is forced. The main lifecycle, pre-filing governance, office-action handling, post-grant maintenance, and cross-cutting withdrawal are clearer as separate composites.
2. Workflow Specifications
BW-PMS-01 — Initial Review Approval Path [Basis]

| Version preservation                                                     | PS and both editable diagram files remain unchanged at v1.2; RR/UC/BR/WF/RTM/Issue-Change Register advance to v1.3. |

| CFR-PMS-006 | Director Assignment and Routing                        | PMS shall route submitted applications to eligible Directors using authorized assignment, expertise, workload and conflict-of-interest criteria, update the review state, and initiate/notify the review timeline.                                                       | Actor-service + Rule/Constraint + Workflow     | 2.3.2 Application Assignment and Routing                            |                 9 |
| CFR-PMS-007 | Director Review and Decision                           | PMS shall support Director review of assigned applications, constrain and record the review decision and justification within the review deadline, route approved applications onward, notify affected Applicants, and audit the review.                                 | Actor-service + Rule/Constraint + Workflow     | 2.3.3 Application Review and Decision                               |                 8 |
| CFR-PMS-008 | Revision and Resubmission                              | PMS shall allow eligible rejected/revision-requested applications to be revised and resubmitted within the permitted period, validate the response to feedback, retain version history, restart review, and expire overdue revisions.                                    | Actor-service + Rule/Constraint + Workflow     | 2.3.4 Application Revision and Resubmission                         |                 7 |
| CFR-PMS-009 | Attorney Assignment                                    | PMS shall support PCC_ADMIN assignment of approved applications to eligible Attorneys, considering approved-panel status, expertise, workload, conflicts and permitted preferences, and shall record Attorney acceptance/decline outcomes.                               | Actor-service + Rule/Constraint + Workflow     | 2.3.5 Attorney Assignment                                           |                 8 |
| CFR-PMS-010 | Patentability and Legal Assessment                     | PMS shall support Attorney legal/patentability assessment, related prior-art and patentability analysis, permitted information/extension requests, recording of a constrained patentability recommendation, and stakeholder notification.                                | Actor-service + Rule/Constraint                | 2.3.6 Patentability Assessment and Legal Review                     |                 8 |
| CFR-PMS-011 | Budget Review and Financial Tracking                   | PMS shall support Director review and decision on patent filing/prosecution budgets, represent required cost components, enforce approval/escalation and availability conditions, and record authorized financial metadata.                                              | Actor-service + Rule/Constraint + Workflow     | 2.3.7 Budget Approval and Financial Tracking                        |                 9 |
| CFR-PMS-012 | External Filing Record and Patent-Office Communication | PMS shall support preparation and recording of domestic/international patent-office filings, validate available filing rules, capture filing identifiers/confirmation, update the application to Filed, and record official patent-office communications.                | Actor-service + Rule/Constraint + Workflow     | 2.3.8 External Filing and Patent Office Communication               |                 8 |
| CFR-PMS-013 | Post-Grant Maintenance                                 | PMS shall support post-grant maintenance tracking for Granted patents, calculate available maintenance schedules, obtain explicit renewal decisions, record approved maintenance actions/history, and schedule subsequent maintenance cycles.                            | Actor-service + Rule/Constraint + Workflow     | 2.3.9 Post-Grant Tracking and Maintenance                           |                 9 |
| CFR-PMS-014 | Office-Action Response Handling                        | PMS shall support recording and review of office actions, preparation and any required Applicant approval of responses, permitted extension handling, response submission recording, status tracking, and deadline monitoring.                                           | Actor-service + Rule/Constraint + Workflow     | 2.3.10 Office Actions and Responses                                 |                 9 |
| CFR-PMS-015 | Pre-Filing Application Withdrawal                      | PMS shall allow an Applicant to withdraw an application before filing only when the current state is withdrawal-eligible, record required reason/consent information, update the state/history, and notify affected parties.                                             | Actor-service + Rule/Constraint + Workflow     | 2.3.11 Application Withdrawal                                       |                 7 |
| CFR-PMS-016 | Co-Inventor and Agreement Management                   | PMS shall support management of inventor/co-inventor information and agreement/consent records, enforce applicable inventor constraints and signature validation, and notify affected parties of inventor-information changes.                                           | Actor-service + Rule/Constraint                | 2.3.12 Co-Inventor and Agreement Management                         |                 8 |
| CFR-PMS-017 | Prior-Art Search and Attachment                        | PMS shall support authorized prior-art searching across available external patent/publication sources, relevance and search aids, attachment of selected references, and audit logging of search sessions.                                                               | Actor-service + Information-access             | 2.3.13 Prior Art Search                                             |                 9 |
| CFR-PMS-018 | Document Version and Access Management                 | PMS shall maintain controlled application-document version history, assign versions, archive prior versions, enforce role/status-based upload/edit/access constraints, and log document modifications.                                                                   | Actor-service + Rule/Constraint                | 2.3.14 Document Version Management                                  |                 8 |
| CFR-PMS-019 | Licensing and Technology-Transfer Records              | PMS shall support submission, routing, negotiation tracking, agreement recording, permitted financial-system information exchange, and confidential record maintenance for licensing/technology-transfer requests.                                                       | Actor-service + Workflow                       | 2.3.15 Patent Licensing and Technology Transfer                     |                 7 |
| CFR-PMS-020 | Application Appeal                                     | PMS shall support timely, justified Applicant appeals of rejected applications, Director review with defined appeal outcomes, outcome recording, and initiation of the applicable next step.                                                                             | Actor-service + Rule/Constraint + Workflow     | 2.3.16 Appeal Process                                               |                 7 |
| CFR-PMS-021 | Deadline Monitoring, Alerts, and Escalation            | PMS shall calculate and monitor supported patent-process deadlines, generate applicable advance alerts and stakeholder notifications, escalate missed deadlines according to configured rules, and log deadline-tracking activity.                                       | System-action + Rule/Constraint + Notification | 2.3.17 Deadline Management and Tracking                             |                10 |
| CFR-PMS-022 | Reports, Dashboards, and Analytics                     | PMS shall provide authorized PMS reports, dashboards and analytics across the stated reporting scope, including supported scheduling, export, customization, historical/trend analysis, and audit logging of report access.                                              | Information-access                             | 2.3.18 Reports and Analytics                                        |                13 |
| CFR-PMS-023 | System-Generated Email Notifications                   | PMS shall trigger system-generated email notifications for the specified patent-management events while treating external email delivery infrastructure as outside PMS scope.                                                                                            | Notification / Boundary                        | 2.3.19 Notifications                                                |                14 |
| CFR-PMS-024 | Audit, Compliance, and Security Controls               | PMS shall maintain auditable records of relevant actions, states and document changes with required audit attributes and immutability for critical operations, provide compliance/forensic access, and apply stated encryption controls at the module/platform boundary. | System-action + Rule/Constraint                | 2.3.20 Audit Trail and Compliance                                   |                 9 |
| CFR-PMS-025 | Role Eligibility for PMS Access                        | PMS shall require a valid assigned PMS role before a user may access patent-management functions.                                                                                                                                                                        | Rule/Constraint                                | 2.3.20 / 2.4                                                        |                 1 |
| CFR-PMS-026 | Lifecycle Authorization Gates                          | PMS shall prevent progression to Attorney assignment or external filing until the corresponding prerequisite approvals and budget allocation required by the accepted lifecycle are complete.                                                                            | Rule/Constraint + Workflow                     | 2.3.3 / 2.3.5 / 2.3.8 / 2.3.21                                      |                 2 |
| CFR-PMS-027 | Confidential Patent Information Access                 | PMS shall enforce role-based access control over confidential patent information.                                                                                                                                                                                        | Rule/Constraint                                | 2.3.20                                                              |                 1 |
| CFR-PMS-028 | PMS Role and Workflow Governance                       | PMS shall enforce the stated PMS role cardinalities/capabilities, status-driven valid transitions, delayed-action escalation, and only explicitly permitted parallel processing.                                                                                         | Rule/Constraint + Workflow                     | 2.3.21 PMS Role, Workflow, Integration, and System Boundaries       |                 7 |
| CFR-PMS-029 | External-System Boundary                               | PMS shall treat patent-office, financial, document-management and prior-art systems as external according to the accepted boundary, recording or querying only the PMS-owned information expressly supported by scope.                                                   | Boundary / Integration                         | 2.3.21 / 2.4                                                        |                 5 |
| CFR-PMS-030 | Budget Rejection Notification Outcome                  | PMS shall allow a Director to reject a proposed patent budget, record that decision, and support the associated notification outcome.                                                                                                                                    | Actor-service + Notification + Workflow        | 2.3.19 Notifications / 2.3.7 Budget Approval and Financial Tracking |                 1 |
